# 2019 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** Who's watching · Continue is the save
**Star:** Disney+ Continue · `itt19-disneyplus` · `sites/disneyplus/home.html`
**ILS June:** table **ends 2018** at 1,630,322,579 · users BLANK · ITU 4.1B / 53.6%
**Netcraft January 2019:** 1,518,207,412 (label January)
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 312 (300 dest-folder leftovers + 12 densify leftovers below)
**Official 10:** frozen in this lock. **Guided 6:** frozen.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt19-disneyplus`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Netcraft + SimilarWeb + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/2019/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/2019/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 2019 — table ends 2018 · ITU 4.1B / 53.6% · bans
2. Disney+ Who's watching — trial never writes
3. TikTok For You — 2019 US mass · not the 2018 merge
4. Apple Arcade — $4.99 · 19 Sep
5. Stadia — Founder's · not 2023 shutdown
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt19-disneyplus`

**Room:** `years/2019/sites/disneyplus/home.html`
**Hook:** Who's watching + Continue · trial is the trap
**Nostalgia:** Disney+ — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- Start 7-day trial never writes.
- 0–1 profiles never write.
- 0–1 Continue titles never write.
- Leftover panel on this room writes a leftover key, **not** `itt19-disneyplus`.

**Complete writes**
- Adult + Kids + ≥2 Continue titles. Writes itt19-disneyplus.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `2019` · key `itt19-disneyplus`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt18-disneyplus` / `itt20-disneyplus` stay empty.
- **Next after gold:** TikTok For You → `years/2019/sites/tiktok/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine · Mickey / Grogu pixels.

## L · Leftover dests (312) — leftover-official

Every leftover dest: trap / 0 ticks / empty / wrong pick never write. Complete writes `{real, leftover, year:"2019"}`. Never writes `itt19-disneyplus`.

### L-0001 · `itt19-disney-lx` · sites/disneyplus/home.html

**Open:** `/years/2019/sites/disneyplus/home.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="disney-lx"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “Who's watching leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `youtube` · `instagram` · `wikipedia`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="disney-lx"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt19-disney-lx']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + wrong pick + Save → “Wrong leftover.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt19-disneyplus` stays empty. `itt18-disney-lx` and `itt20-disney-lx` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click keep leftover pick if present. Wrong pick never writes.
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “Who's watching leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="disney-lx"]`.
- `localStorage['itt19-disney-lx']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2019", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt19-disney-lx`.
- Does **not** write `itt19-disneyplus`.
- **Next:** `/years/2019/sites/disneyplus/home.html`

**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="tb-lx"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “post leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `youtube` · `instagram` · `wikipedia`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="tb-lx"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt19-tb-lx']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + wrong pick + Save → “Wrong leftover.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).

- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “post leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="tb-lx"]`.
- `localStorage['itt19-tb-lx']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2019", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt19-tb-lx`.
